    "id": "Bitru atau Sitri adalah pangeran agung neraka dalam demonologi, berwujud macan tutul bersayap griffin, yang mengobarkan nafsu.",
    "en": "Bitru or Sitri is a great prince of hell in demonology, a leopard with griffin's wings who inflames lust.",

    {
      "id": "Bitru, dikenal juga sebagai Sidragaso, Sytry, atau Sitri, adalah pangeran agung neraka dalam demonologi. Ia berwujud macan tutul bersayap griffin tetapi dapat muncul sebagai manusia yang sangat tampan, dan memimpin 70 legiun. Tugasnya mengobarkan nafsu antara lelaki dan perempuan, dan ia suka membuka rahasia perempuan untuk mempermalukan mereka.",
      "en": "Bitru, also known as Sidragaso, Sytry or Sitri, is a great prince of hell in demonology. He has the form of a leopard with griffin's wings but can appear as a very handsome human, and commands 70 legions. His task is to inflame lust between men and women, and he likes revealing women's secrets to ridicule them.",

      "quote": "Bitru es un personaje perteneciente a la demonología. También es conocido como Sidragaso, Sytry o Sitri",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Bitru adalah tokoh demonologi yang juga dikenal sebagai Sidragaso, Sytry, atau Sitri.",
        "en": "Bitru is a figure of demonology also known as Sidragaso, Sytry or Sitri."

    {
      "id": "bitru-c02",
      "source_id": "bitru-s1",
      "quote": "Gran príncipe de los infiernos, tiene la forma de un leopardo con alas de grifo, pero también puede aparecer bajo una forma humana muy hermosa. Comanda 70 legiones",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia pangeran agung neraka berwujud macan tutul bersayap griffin, yang juga dapat muncul sebagai manusia sangat tampan, dan memimpin 70 legiun.",
        "en": "A great prince of hell, he has the form of a leopard with griffin's wings but can also appear as a very handsome human, and commands 70 legions."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bitru-c03",
      "source_id": "bitru-s1",
      "quote": "es el encargado de inflamar de lujuria al hombre hacia la mujer y viceversa. Descubre, cuando le parece, los secretos de las mujeres, a quienes siempre pone en ridículo.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Tugasnya mengobarkan nafsu lelaki terhadap perempuan dan sebaliknya; bila ia mau, ia membuka rahasia perempuan dan selalu mempermalukan mereka.",
        "en": "His task is to inflame men with lust for women and vice versa; when he pleases he reveals women's secrets, always making them look ridiculous."
      }
    }

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Bocarrot adalah roh ladang gandum dalam mitologi Katalan, kerabat satir dan faun, yang bersembunyi di bulir terakhir sampai tertangkap sabit.",
    "en": "The bocarrot is a wheat-field spirit of Catalan mythology, akin to satyrs and fauns, that hides in the last ears until caught by the sickle.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "bocarrot-c01",
      "bocarrot-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Bocarrot adalah roh mitologi Katalan yang berkerabat dengan satir dan faun. Menurut kepercayaan lama ia tinggal di ladang gandum dan membuat bulirnya besar dan melimpah. Ia tidak terlihat dan dapat membesar atau mengecil sesuka hati. Penuai yang tiba-tiba sakit dianggap tersandung bocarrot, yang bersembunyi di antara bulir sampai tertangkap ayunan sabit terakhir.",
      "en": "The bocarrot is a spirit of Catalan mythology akin to satyrs and fauns. In old belief it lived in wheat fields and made the grain big and plentiful. It is invisible and can grow or shrink at will. A reaper who suddenly fell ill was thought to have stumbled on the bocarrot, which hid among the ears until caught by the last stroke of the sickle.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "bocarrot-c01",
        "bocarrot-c02",
        "bocarrot-c03"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Di Samalús gandum terakhir disebut bocarrot, dan di Gósol para penuai percaya siapa yang menangkap roh gandum terakhir ini akan mendapat panen baik dan kemakmuran.",
      "en": "In Samalús the last wheat was called the bocarrot, and in Gósol reapers believed that whoever caught this spirit of the last wheat would gain a good harvest and prosperity.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "bocarrot-c04",
        "bocarrot-c05"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "cultural_context": null,
  "etymology": null,

  "sources": [
    {
      "id": "bocarrot-s1",

      "title": "Bocarrot",
      "author": null,

      "published": null,
      "language": "ca",
      "type": "wikipedia",
      "accessed": "2026-10-02"
    }
  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "bocarrot-c01",
      "source_id": "bocarrot-s1",
      "quote": "El bocarrot és una mena de geni propi de la mitologia catalana. Emparentat amb els sàtirs grecs i els faunes romans, segons les antigues creences populars vivia als camps de blat i n'estimulava el creixement de les plantes",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Bocarrot adalah semacam roh mitologi Katalan yang berkerabat dengan satir Yunani dan faun Romawi; menurut kepercayaan lama ia tinggal di ladang gandum dan merangsang pertumbuhan tanamannya.",
        "en": "The bocarrot is a kind of genie of Catalan mythology related to the Greek satyrs and Roman fauns; in old popular belief it lived in wheat fields and spurred the plants' growth."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bocarrot-c02",
      "source_id": "bocarrot-s1",
      "quote": "Si un segador es trobava malalt de forma sobtada era perquè havia ensopegat amb el bocarrot, que s'anava amagant entre les espigues fins que era capturat amb l'últim cop de falç.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Bila seorang penuai tiba-tiba sakit, itu karena ia tersandung bocarrot, yang bersembunyi di antara bulir gandum sampai tertangkap pada ayunan sabit terakhir.",
        "en": "If a reaper suddenly fell ill it was because he had stumbled on the bocarrot, which hid among the ears of wheat until caught with the last stroke of the sickle."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bocarrot-c03",
      "source_id": "bocarrot-s1",
      "quote": "El bocarrot és invisible i es pot fer més gran o més petit a voluntat.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Bocarrot tidak terlihat dan dapat membesar atau mengecil sesuka hati.",
        "en": "The bocarrot is invisible and can grow larger or smaller at will."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bocarrot-c04",
      "source_id": "bocarrot-s1",
      "quote": "A Samalús, Vallès Oriental, es coneixia com a \"bocarrot\" el darrer blat, mentre que de la darrera mota de blat que quedava per segar se'n deia \"boc\" o \"cabra\".",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Samalús, Vallès Oriental, gandum terakhir disebut bocarrot, dan rumpun terakhir yang belum dituai disebut boc (kambing jantan) atau cabra (kambing betina).",
        "en": "In Samalús, Vallès Oriental, the last wheat was called the bocarrot, and the last clump left to reap was called the boc (billy goat) or cabra (nanny goat)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "bocarrot-c05",
      "source_id": "bocarrot-s1",
      "quote": "A Gósol, Berguedà, els antics segadors creien en un geni del blat que anomenaven \"el boc\", l'esperit del darrer blat. Qui agafava el \"bocarrot\" s'assegurava el rendiment del camp, riquesa i prosperitat.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Gósol, Berguedà, para penuai percaya pada roh gandum bernama el boc, roh gandum terakhir; siapa yang menangkap bocarrot menjamin hasil ladang, kekayaan, dan kemakmuran.",
        "en": "In Gósol, Berguedà, reapers believed in a wheat spirit called el boc, the spirit of the last wheat; whoever caught the bocarrot secured the field's yield, wealth and prosperity."
      }
    }
  ],

      "id": "Caacrinolaas, lebih dikenal sebagai Glasya-Labolas, adalah Presiden neraka yang memimpin tiga puluh enam legiun iblis. Ia digambarkan sebagai anjing bersayap griffin.   "claims": [
    {
      "id": "caacrinolaas-c01",
      "source_id": "caacrinolaas-s1",
      "quote": "Na demonologia, Glasya-Labolas (também grafado Caacrinolaas, Caassimolar, Classyalabolas, Glassia-labolis ou Glasya Labolas) é poderoso Presidente (e Fidalgo para outros autores) do Inferno e comanda trinta e seis legiões de demônios.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam demonologi, Glasya-Labolas (juga ditulis Caacrinolaas, Caassimolar, atau Classyalabolas) adalah Presiden neraka yang berkuasa dan memimpin tiga puluh enam legiun iblis.",
        "en": "In demonology Glasya-Labolas (also spelled Caacrinolaas, Caassimolar or Classyalabolas) is a mighty President of hell commanding thirty-six legions of demons."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "caacrinolaas-c02",
      "source_id": "caacrinolaas-s1",
      "quote": "é descrito como o Grande Presidente do Inferno, que pode conceder talento nas Belas Artes e inspirar nos homens uma grande sede de sangue.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia digambarkan sebagai Presiden Agung neraka yang dapat memberi bakat seni rupa dan membangkitkan haus darah pada manusia.",
        "en": "He is described as the Great President of hell, able to grant talent in the fine arts and inspire a great thirst for blood in men."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "caacrinolaas-c03",
      "source_id": "caacrinolaas-s1",
      "quote": "Ele é o capitão e autor, no que diz respeito aos homicídios e derramamento de sangue, diz a todos, as coisas sobre o passado e no que há de acontecer",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia pemimpin dan biang pembunuhan serta pertumpahan darah, dan memberi tahu hal-hal masa lalu dan masa depan.",
        "en": "He is the captain and author of murders and bloodshed, and tells of things past and to come."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "caacrinolaas-c04",
      "source_id": "caacrinolaas-s1",
      "quote": "causando-lhes o amor entre eles, e se o desejar, incita a cometer homicídios e pode fazer de um homem invisível.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Cabicanca adalah burung legendaris dalam tradisi Portugal, burung berparuh aneh yang menakuti warga Aguiar sampai ditembak.",
    "en": "The cabicanca is a legendary bird of Portuguese tradition, a strange-beaked bird that frightened the people of Aguiar until it was shot.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "cabicanca-c01",
      "cabicanca-c02",
      "cabicanca-c04"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Cabicanca adalah makhluk legendaris berwujud burung dalam tradisi Portugal. Menurut legenda, seekor burung yang belum pernah terlihat hinggap di menara gereja Aguiar, dan penduduk yang takut berhenti pergi ke misa sambil berseru tentang paruhnya. Pengangkut barang Martinho Afonso akhirnya menembak burung itu, yang ternyata seekor bangau, dan ia diarak di atas bahu. Nama cabicanca kini melekat pada warga Aguiar.",
      "en": "The cabicanca is a legendary bird-shaped creature of Portuguese tradition. In the legend a bird never seen before landed on the church tower of Aguiar, and the frightened locals stopped going to Mass, exclaiming at its beak. The muleteer Martinho Afonso finally shot the bird, which turned out to be a stork, and was carried shoulder-high. The name cabicanca now belongs to the people of Aguiar.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "cabicanca-c01",
        "cabicanca-c02",
        "cabicanca-c03",
        "cabicanca-c04",
        "cabicanca-c05"
      ]

  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "cabicanca-c01",
      "source_id": "cabicanca-s1",
      "quote": "A cabicanca é uma criatura lendária com forma de pássaro das tradições e superstições populares portuguesas.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Cabicanca adalah makhluk legendaris berwujud burung dalam tradisi dan takhayul rakyat Portugal.",
        "en": "The cabicanca is a legendary bird-shaped creature of Portuguese popular tradition and superstition."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cabicanca-c02",
      "source_id": "cabicanca-s1",
      "quote": "Pousou um dia na torre da Igreja um pássaro, jamais visto por terras de Aguiar. As gentes do lugar deixaram de ir à missa",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Suatu hari seekor burung yang belum pernah terlihat di Aguiar hinggap di menara gereja, dan penduduk berhenti pergi ke misa.",
        "en": "One day a bird never before seen in Aguiar landed on the church tower, and the locals stopped going to Mass."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cabicanca-c03",
      "source_id": "cabicanca-s1",
      "quote": "Que bicanca, Santo Deus, que bicanca!",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Orang-orang berseru \"Que bicanca!\" (\"Paruh yang luar biasa!\"), asal nama cabicanca.",
        "en": "People exclaimed \"Que bicanca!\" (\"What a beak!\"), the source of the name cabicanca."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cabicanca-c04",
      "source_id": "cabicanca-s1",
      "quote": "Bastou um tiro certeiro, que ecoou por toda a região, para que a cegonha sucumbisse e Afonso fosse erguido em ombros.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Seorang pengangkut barang bernama Martinho Afonso menembaknya; satu tembakan tepat menjatuhkan bangau itu, dan Afonso diarak di atas bahu.",
        "en": "A muleteer named Martinho Afonso shot it; one accurate shot brought down the stork, and Afonso was carried shoulder-high."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cabicanca-c05",
      "source_id": "cabicanca-s1",
      "quote": "A história perdura e o nome \"cabicanca\" ficou para sempre ligado aos aguiarenses que, carinhosamente, o adoptaram como seu.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kisah ini bertahan dan nama cabicanca melekat pada warga Aguiar, yang mengadopsinya dengan sayang.",
        "en": "The story endures, and the name cabicanca has stuck to the people of Aguiar, who fondly adopted it as their own."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Cabraboc adalah makhluk setengah manusia setengah kambing dalam imajinasi rakyat Katalan yang mengejar pejalan malam di hutan.",
    "en": "The cabraboc is a half-human, half-goat being of Catalan folk imagination that chases night travellers in the forest.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "cabraboc-c01",
      "cabraboc-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Cabraboc adalah makhluk setengah manusia di bagian atas dan setengah kambing di bagian bawah. Mereka makhluk hutan yang mengejar pejalan malam, sering sampai korban terjatuh dari tebing, dan kadang digambarkan bersayap kelelawar.",
      "en": "The cabraboc is a being that is human above and goat below. They are forest creatures that chase night travellers, often until the victim falls off a cliff, and are sometimes described with bat wings.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "cabraboc-c01",
        "cabraboc-c02",
        "cabraboc-c03"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Dalam legenda Ripollet, seekor kucing keluar dari keranjang sepasang orang tua lalu berubah menjadi iblis Cabraboc. Legenda ini dipentaskan pada karnaval Ripollet, dan kelompok Diables de Caldes de Montbui menamai cabraboc helm berkepala kambing iblis utama mereka.",
      "en": "In a Ripollet legend a cat came out of an old couple's basket and turned into the devil Cabraboc. The legend is staged at the Ripollet carnival, and the Diables de Caldes de Montbui call their main devil's goat-headed helmet a cabraboc.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "cabraboc-c04",
        "cabraboc-c05",
        "cabraboc-c06"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "cabraboc-c01",
      "source_id": "cabraboc-s1",
      "quote": "Un cabraboc és un ésser fantàstic propi de l'imaginari popular català, mig humà per la part de dalt i mig cabra o boc per la part de baix.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Cabraboc adalah makhluk khayalan imajinasi rakyat Katalan, setengah manusia di bagian atas dan setengah kambing di bagian bawah.",
        "en": "A cabraboc is a fantastic being of Catalan folk imagination, half human above and half goat below."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cabraboc-c02",
      "source_id": "cabraboc-s1",
      "quote": "Es diu que els cabrabocs persegueixen els homes com si fossin cabres i les dones com si fossin bocs. Són éssers del bosc i cal evitar-los quan es viatja de nit",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Para cabraboc konon mengejar lelaki seperti kambing betina dan perempuan seperti kambing jantan; mereka makhluk hutan yang harus dihindari saat bepergian malam.",
        "en": "Cabrabocs are said to chase men as if they were nanny goats and women as if they were billy goats; they are forest beings to be avoided when travelling at night."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cabraboc-c03",
      "source_id": "cabraboc-s1",
      "quote": "sovint, les persecucions acaben fent estimbar la víctima per un penya-segat. Algunes vegades, se'ls descrivia amb ales de ratapinyada.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pengejaran itu sering berakhir dengan korban terjatuh dari tebing, dan kadang mereka digambarkan bersayap kelelawar.",
        "en": "The chases often end with the victim falling off a cliff, and they were sometimes described with bat wings."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cabraboc-c04",
      "source_id": "cabraboc-s1",
      "quote": "Aleshores, del cistell en sortí un gat que es convertí en un diable més, el Cabraboc.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam legenda Ripollet El vell i la vella, seekor kucing keluar dari keranjang sepasang orang tua dan berubah menjadi iblis, si Cabraboc.",
        "en": "In the Ripollet legend El vell i la vella, a cat came out of an old couple's basket and turned into one more devil, the Cabraboc."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cabraboc-c05",
      "source_id": "cabraboc-s1",
      "quote": "Els Diables de Caldes de Montbui donen el nom de cabraboc al casc que porta el diable principal, que anomenen Llucifer.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kelompok Diables de Caldes de Montbui menamai cabraboc helm yang dipakai iblis utama mereka, Llucifer.",
        "en": "The Diables de Caldes de Montbui give the name cabraboc to the helmet worn by their main devil, Llucifer."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cabraboc-c06",
      "source_id": "cabraboc-s1",
      "quote": "Les Bruixes de Ripollet n'escenifiquen la llegenda durant el carnaval infantil, i els Diables de Ripollet el treuen al capdavant de la rua de Carnaval de dissabte.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Les Bruixes de Ripollet mementaskan legenda ini pada karnaval anak, dan Diables de Ripollet membawanya di depan pawai karnaval hari Sabtu.",
        "en": "Les Bruixes de Ripollet stage the legend at the children's carnival, and the Diables de Ripollet carry it at the head of Saturday's carnival parade."
      }
    }
  ],

    "id": "Cagrino adalah roh jahat dalam cerita rakyat Rom, berwujud landak kuning, yang menunggangi kuda pada malam hari sampai kelelahan.",
    "en": "The Cagrino is an evil spirit of the folklore of the Roma, shaped like a yellow hedgehog, that rides horses to exhaustion at night.",

    {
      "id": "Cagrino atau Guecubu adalah roh jahat dalam cerita rakyat orang Rom di daratan Eropa, yang mitosnya berasal dari barat laut India dengan nama Harginn. Ia berwujud landak kuning sepanjang sekitar 50 cm. Pada malam hari ia menunggangi kuda sampai kelelahan, sehingga esok harinya kuda itu sakit, surainya kusut, dan basah oleh keringat. Sebagai penangkal, kuda diikat pada tiang yang digosok bawang putih dengan benang merah berbentuk salib.",
      "en": "The Cagrino or Guecubu is an evil spirit in the folklore of the continental Roma whose myth comes from north-western India, where it is called Harginn. It has the form of a yellow hedgehog about 50 cm long. At night it rides horses to exhaustion, so the next day they are sick, tangled-maned and drenched in sweat. As a remedy, horses are tied to a garlic-rubbed stake with a red thread laid in a cross.",

      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Cagrino, dikenal juga sebagai Guecubu, adalah roh jahat dalam cerita rakyat orang Rom di daratan Eropa.",
        "en": "The Cagrino, also known as Guecubu, is an evil spirit in the folklore of the continental Roma."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cagrino-c02",
      "source_id": "cagrino-s1",
      "quote": "Il mito ha origine dalle regioni nord-occidentali dell'India, dove lo spirito viene chiamato Harginn .",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mitos ini berasal dari wilayah barat laut India, tempat roh itu disebut Harginn.",
        "en": "The myth originates in north-western India, where the spirit is called Harginn."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cagrino-c03",
      "source_id": "cagrino-s1",
      "quote": "Ha la forma di un riccio giallo, lungo circa 50 cm, largo 20. Cavalca i cavalli fino allo sfinimento nella notte",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia berwujud landak kuning sepanjang sekitar 50 cm dan lebar 20 cm, dan menunggangi kuda pada malam hari sampai kuda itu kelelahan.",
        "en": "It has the form of a yellow hedgehog about 50 cm long and 20 wide, and rides horses to exhaustion at night."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cagrino-c04",
      "source_id": "cagrino-s1",
      "quote": "il giorno dopo appaiono malati ed esausti, con la criniera arruffata e in un bagno di sudore.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Esok harinya kuda-kuda itu tampak sakit dan lelah, surainya kusut, dan basah kuyup oleh keringat.",
        "en": "The next day the horses look sick and exhausted, with tangled manes and drenched in sweat."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cagrino-c05",
      "source_id": "cagrino-s1",
      "quote": "i cavalli vengono legati a un bastone su cui è stato precedentemente strofinato dell'aglio, ai piedi del quale viene posto un filo rosso formante una croce",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Untuk menangkalnya, kuda diikat pada tiang yang telah digosok bawang putih, dengan benang merah berbentuk salib di kakinya.",
        "en": "As a remedy, the horses are tied to a stake rubbed with garlic, with a red thread forming a cross at its foot."
      }
    }
  ],

    "id": "Calygreyhound adalah makhluk campuran heraldik abad pertengahan yang dipakai keluarga de Vere, Earl of Oxford, sebagai lambang kecepatan.",
    "en": "The calygreyhound is a hybrid creature of medieval heraldry used by the de Vere Earls of Oxford, said to represent speed.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "calygreyhound-c01",
      "calygreyhound-c04"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Calygreyhound adalah makhluk mitos dalam heraldik abad pertengahan yang dipakai keluarga de Vere, Earl of Oxford, pada abad ke-15 dan ke-16, dan mungkin khas keluarga itu. Ia berkepala kucing liar, bertubuh rusa atau antelop, bercakar elang di kaki depan, berkuku lembu, bertanduk, berkaki belakang singa, dan berekor singa atau pudel; sesekali ia bersayap elang.",
      "en": "The calygreyhound is a mythical creature of medieval heraldry used by the de Vere Earls of Oxford in the 15th and 16th centuries, and possibly unique to them. It has a wildcat's head, a deer's or antelope's body, eagle's claws on its forefeet, ox hooves, horns, a lion's hind legs and a lion's or poodle's tail; occasionally it has eagle's wings.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "calygreyhound-c01",
        "calygreyhound-c02",
        "calygreyhound-c03",
        "calygreyhound-c04"

    {
      "id": "Ia konon melambangkan kecepatan. Tidak seperti kebanyakan monster heraldik, ia tidak berusaha tampak realistis, dan umumnya dianggap rekaan seniman.",
      "en": "It is said to represent speed. Unlike most heraldic monsters it makes no attempt at realism and is generally thought to be the artist's invention.",
      "claim_ids": [

      "quote": "The calygreyhound is a mythical creature that appears on medieval heraldry. The de Vere family, who were the Earls of Oxford, used the calygreyhound in their coat of arms in the 15th and 16th centuries.",
      "locator": null,

      "statement": {
        "id": "Calygreyhound adalah makhluk mitos dalam heraldik abad pertengahan; keluarga de Vere, Earl of Oxford, memakainya pada lambang mereka pada abad ke-15 dan ke-16.",

    {
      "id": "calygreyhound-c02",
      "source_id": "calygreyhound-s1",
      "quote": "The calygreyhound may be unique to the de Veres, and unlike most heraldic monsters, it made no attempt to seem realistic.",

      "statement": {
        "id": "Calygreyhound mungkin khas keluarga de Vere, dan tidak seperti kebanyakan monster heraldik, ia tidak berusaha tampak realistis.",
        "en": "The calygreyhound may be unique to the de Veres, and unlike most heraldic monsters it made no attempt to look realistic."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "calygreyhound-c03",
      "source_id": "calygreyhound-s1",
      "quote": "The calygreyhound is described consistently as having the head of a wildcat, the torso of a deer or antelope, the claws of an eagle on its forefeet, ox hooves, antlers or horns, the hind legs of a lion or ox, and its tail like a lion or poodle.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia digambarkan berkepala kucing liar, bertubuh rusa atau antelop, bercakar elang di kaki depan, berkuku lembu, bertanduk, berkaki belakang singa atau lembu, dan berekor seperti singa atau pudel.",
        "en": "It is consistently described with a wildcat's head, a deer's or antelope's body, eagle's claws on the forefeet, ox hooves, antlers or horns, a lion's or ox's hind legs, and a tail like a lion's or poodle's."
      }

      "source_id": "calygreyhound-s1",
      "quote": "On some rare occasions, the Calygreyhound may be seen as depicted with the wings of an eagle. It is said to represent speed or swiftness.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sesekali ia digambarkan bersayap elang, dan konon melambangkan kecepatan.",
        "en": "Occasionally it is shown with eagle's wings, and it is said to represent speed or swiftness."
      }
    },

      "id": "calygreyhound-c05",
      "source_id": "calygreyhound-s1",
      "quote": "It is generally accepted that the calygreyhound is simply a figment of the artist's imagination, but some parts of it are based on reality.",

    },
    {
      "id": "caquesseitao-c03",
      "source_id": "caquesseitao-s1",
      "quote": "com pescoço de cobra e uma unha, a modo de esporão de galo na testa, com rabo muito comprido pintado de verde e preto, como são os lagartos desta terra.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia berleher ular, bertaji seperti ayam jantan di dahinya, dan berekor sangat panjang bercorak hijau dan hitam seperti kadal di negeri itu.",
        "en": "It has a snake's neck, a cockspur-like claw on its forehead, and a very long green-and-black tail like the lizards of that land."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "caquesseitao-c04",
      "source_id": "caquesseitao-s1",
      "quote": "Estes bichos de voo, a modo de salto, caçam os bugios e bichos por cima das árvores, dos quais se mantêm.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Hewan ini terbang dengan cara melompat dan memburu monyet serta hewan lain di atas pohon sebagai makanannya.",
        "en": "These creatures fly in leaps and hunt monkeys and other animals in the treetops for food."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "caquesseitao-c05",
      "source_id": "caquesseitao-s1",
      "quote": "Tornou-se presença frequente na arte indo-portuguesa.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Caquesseitão sering muncul dalam seni Indo-Portugis.",
        "en": "The Caquesseitão became a frequent presence in Indo-Portuguese art."
      }
    }
  ],

    "id": "Cardapeçols adalah raksasa perempuan dari sekitar Montserrat yang menyisir anak perempuan dengan sisir wol sampai botak.",
    "en": "The Cardapeçols is an ogress from around Montserrat who combs girls with wool cards until they are bald.",

      "id": "Cardapeçols adalah momok penakut anak dari sekitar Montserrat, raksasa perempuan berwajah garang yang menghukum anak perempuan yang tidak mau disisir dengan menyentak rambut mereka hingga tercabut. Namanya berasal dari sisir wol yang dipakainya. Joan Amades mencatat pada 1957 bahwa ia dikenal di El Bruc, Esparraguera, dan Piera.",
      "en": "The Cardapeçols is a child-scaring bogey from around Montserrat, a scowling ogress who punishes girls who refuse to be combed by tearing their hair out. Her name comes from the wool cards she uses. Joan Amades recorded in 1957 that she was known in El Bruc, Esparraguera and Piera.",

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "cardapecols-c01",
      "source_id": "cardapecols-s1",
      "quote": "La Cardapeçols és una espantamainades pròpia de l'imaginari popular català, concretament de la rodalia de Montserrat.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Cardapeçols adalah momok penakut anak imajinasi rakyat Katalan dari sekitar Montserrat.",
        "en": "The Cardapeçols is a child-scaring bogey of Catalan folk imagination from around Montserrat."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cardapecols-c02",
      "source_id": "cardapecols-s1",
      "quote": "Es tracta d'una ogressa malcarada que castiga les nenes que no es deixen pentinar (tal com fa també la Moixina, una altra espantamainades semblant) i els fa unes estrebades tan fortes que els arrenca els cabells",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia raksasa perempuan berwajah garang yang menghukum anak perempuan yang tidak mau disisir, seperti Moixina, dengan menyentak rambut mereka begitu keras hingga tercabut.",
        "en": "She is a scowling ogress who punishes girls who will not let their hair be combed, like the Moixina, tugging so hard she tears their hair out."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cardapecols-c03",
      "source_id": "cardapecols-s1",
      "quote": "El nom li ve del fet que per a pentinar les seves víctimes se serveix de cards i aurons per cardar llana d'ovella",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Namanya berasal dari sisir wol (card) yang dipakainya untuk menyisir korbannya.",
        "en": "Her name comes from the wool cards she uses to comb her victims."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cardapecols-c04",
      "source_id": "cardapecols-s1",
      "quote": "Segons va recollir Joan Amades en un article a la Revista de Dialectología y Tradiciones Populares el 1957, la Cardapeçols és coneguda al Bruc, Esparraguera i Piera.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut catatan Joan Amades dalam Revista de Dialectología y Tradiciones Populares tahun 1957, Cardapeçols dikenal di El Bruc, Esparraguera, dan Piera.",
        "en": "According to Joan Amades in the Revista de Dialectología y Tradiciones Populares in 1957, the Cardapeçols is known in El Bruc, Esparraguera and Piera."
      }
    }

    "en": "The carro da condeceira is the witches' cart of the folklore of Guimarães that carries the devil and witches by night.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "carro-da-condeceira-c01",
      "carro-da-condeceira-c02"
    ]

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Carro da condeceira adalah kereta para penyihir yang membawa iblis dan penyihir yang menari di dalamnya. Ia hanya berjalan pada malam hari dan berderit keras saat melewati desa. Suatu kali di Rua dos Gatos, Guimarães, seorang yang penasaran melongok dari jendela dan langsung mendapat tamparan dahsyat.",
      "en": "The carro da condeceira is the witches' cart, carrying the devil and dancing witches. It travels only at night and creaks loudly through the villages. Once in the Rua dos Gatos in Guimarães a curious man looked out of his window and was at once given a tremendous slap.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "carro-da-condeceira-c01",
        "carro-da-condeceira-c02",
        "carro-da-condeceira-c03"

      "source_id": "carro-da-condeceira-s1",
      "quote": "O carro da condeceira é o carro das bruxas. O carro conduz o diabo e as bruxas que vão dentro a dançar. O carro faz uma grande chiadeira ao passar pelas aldeias.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Carro da condeceira adalah kereta para penyihir yang membawa iblis dan penyihir yang menari di dalamnya, dan berderit keras saat melewati desa.",
        "en": "The carro da condeceira is the witches' cart, carrying the devil and the witches dancing inside, and creaking loudly as it passes through villages."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "carro-da-condeceira-c02",
      "source_id": "carro-da-condeceira-s1",
      "quote": "O carro da condeceira é o carro das Bruxas, que anda só de noite. Uma vez ia ele a passar pela Rua-dos-gatos (Guimarães), e um curioso veio à janela ver o que era",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kereta itu hanya berjalan malam hari; suatu kali saat melewati Rua dos Gatos di Guimarães, seorang yang penasaran melongok dari jendela.",
        "en": "The cart travels only at night; once, as it passed along the Rua dos Gatos in Guimarães, a curious man came to the window to look."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "carro-da-condeceira-c03",
      "source_id": "carro-da-condeceira-s1",
      "quote": "despertado pela grande chiadeira que o carro fazia: imediatamente levou uma bofetada monumental.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Orang yang terbangun oleh deritnya itu langsung mendapat tamparan dahsyat.",
        "en": "Woken by the cart's loud creaking, he immediately received a tremendous slap."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Cavalum adalah iblis berwujud kuda bersayap kelelawar dari legenda Madeira, yang dipenjara Tuhan di gua Furnas do Cavalum.",
    "en": "The Cavalum is a bat-winged, horse-shaped devil of a legend of Madeira, imprisoned by God in the Furnas do Cavalum caves.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "cavalum-c01",
      "cavalum-c02",
      "cavalum-c05"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Cavalum adalah iblis berwujud kuda, bersayap kelelawar, dan menyemburkan api dari hidungnya, yang menurut legenda tinggal di Furnas do Cavalum, gua basal di Machico, Madeira.",
      "en": "The Cavalum is a horse-shaped devil with bat wings that breathes fire from its nostrils, said to live in the Furnas do Cavalum, basalt caves in Machico, Madeira.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "cavalum-c01",
        "cavalum-c02"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Ia pernah mendatangi gereja dan menantang Tuhan bahwa ia akan menghancurkan desa, lalu mendatangkan badai besar sambil meringkik gembira dari puncak tebing. Tuhan akhirnya mengusir badai dan memenjarakannya di Furnas, tempat ringkikan marahnya masih terdengar saat badai besar.",
      "en": "He once came to a church and challenged God that he would destroy the village, then raised a great storm, neighing with glee from the clifftop. God finally drove away the storm and imprisoned him in the Furnas, where his angry neighing is still heard during big storms.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "cavalum-c03",
        "cavalum-c04",
        "cavalum-c05"
      ]
    }

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "cavalum-c01",
      "source_id": "cavalum-s1",
      "quote": "A Lenda do Cavalum é uma lenda acerca das Furnas do Cavalum, situadas na vila de Machico, na ilha da Madeira. As Furnas são umas grandes grutas escavadas em basalto que o saber popular diz que são a moradia dum monstro.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Legenda Cavalum berkisah tentang Furnas do Cavalum di Machico, pulau Madeira, gua besar di batuan basal yang menurut kepercayaan rakyat adalah tempat tinggal monster.",
        "en": "The legend of the Cavalum concerns the Furnas do Cavalum in Machico on Madeira, large caves in basalt that popular lore says are a monster's home."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cavalum-c02",
      "source_id": "cavalum-s1",
      "quote": "O Cavalum é um diabo com a forma dum cavalo, com asas de morcego e que solta fogo das narinas.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Cavalum adalah iblis berwujud kuda, bersayap kelelawar, dan menyemburkan api dari lubang hidungnya.",
        "en": "The Cavalum is a devil in the shape of a horse, with bat wings, that breathes fire from its nostrils."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cavalum-c03",
      "source_id": "cavalum-s1",
      "quote": "foi bater à porta duma igreja para falar com Deus. No momento que Deus lhe inquiriu o porquê dele ter ido falar com ele, o Cavalum respondeu que queria destruir toda a povoação e espicaçou-O a frustá-lo.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Cavalum mengetuk pintu gereja untuk berbicara dengan Tuhan dan berkata ingin menghancurkan seluruh desa, menantang Tuhan untuk menggagalkannya.",
        "en": "The Cavalum told God he wanted to destroy the whole village, daring Him to thwart it."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cavalum-c04",
      "source_id": "cavalum-s1",
      "quote": "O Cavalum reuniu então as trevas contra a povoação resultando numa grande tempestade. Do alto do penhasco, o Cavalum relinchava com regozijo defronte do desespero dos moradores.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Cavalum mengumpulkan kegelapan melawan desa hingga terjadi badai besar, dan dari puncak tebing ia meringkik gembira melihat keputusasaan warga.",
        "en": "The Cavalum gathered darkness against the village, causing a great storm, and neighed with glee from the clifftop at the villagers' despair."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cavalum-c05",
      "source_id": "cavalum-s1",
      "quote": "Para que o monstro não voltasse a causar estragos, Deus resolveu prender o Cavalum nas Furnas, onde ainda hoje de tempos a tempos se ouve o seu relhinchar zangado contra Deus, durante as tempestades fortes.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Agar ia tidak lagi merusak, Tuhan memenjarakan Cavalum di Furnas, tempat ringkikan marahnya masih terdengar saat badai besar.",
        "en": "So the monster would do no more harm, God imprisoned the Cavalum in the Furnas, where its angry neighing is still heard during strong storms."
      }
    }
  ],

    "id": "Cenocroca adalah hewan legendaris bersuara manusia, berahang singa dan bertubuh keledai, yang meniru suara orang untuk memikat mangsa.",
    "en": "The Cenocroca is a legendary beast with a human voice, a lion's jaws and an ass's body, that mimics people to lure prey.",

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Cenocroca adalah hewan legendaris yang sering tertukar dengan Crocotta. Ia bersuara manusia, giginya satu tulang utuh, mulutnya terbelah sampai telinga, berahang dan berdada singa, bertubuh keledai, dan bertanduk ibex. Pupilnya tak pernah bergerak, dan ia meniru panggilan orang atau suara hewan untuk memikat mangsa.",
      "en": "The Cenocroca is a legendary beast often confused with the Crocotta. It has a human voice, a solid bone for teeth, a mouth slit to its ears, a lion's jaws and chest, an ass's body and ibex horns. Its pupils never move, and it imitates human calls or animal cries to lure prey.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "cenocroca-c01",
        "cenocroca-c02",
        "cenocroca-c03"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Sebutan tertua makhluk bernama Krokottas ada dalam Geografi karya Strabo, sebagai persilangan anjing dan serigala dari Etiopia. Makhluk ini juga digambarkan dalam Il nome della rosa karya Umberto Eco.",
      "en": "The oldest mention of a creature called Krokottas is in Strabo's Geography, as a dog-wolf cross from Ethiopia. The creature also appears in Umberto Eco's Il nome della rosa.",

  "sources": [
    {
      "id": "cenocroca-s1",
      "url": "https://it.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cenocroca",
      "title": "Cenocroca",

      "publisher": "Wikipedia (it)",
      "published": null,

      "accessed": "2026-10-02"
    }
  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "cenocroca-c01",
      "source_id": "cenocroca-s1",
      "quote": "La bestia Cenocroca è un animale immaginario o una creatura leggendaria. La creatura era spesso e reciprocamente confusa con la Crocotta e chiamata anche Crocuta o Crocotte.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Cenocroca adalah hewan khayalan atau makhluk legendaris yang sering tertukar dengan Crocotta, dan juga disebut Crocuta atau Crocotte.",
        "en": "The Cenocroca is an imaginary animal or legendary creature often confused with the Crocotta, also called Crocuta or Crocotte."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cenocroca-c02",
      "source_id": "cenocroca-s1",
      "quote": "Viene descritta come avente la voce umana ma al posto dei denti un solido osso; un taglio della bocca che arriva fino alle orecchie, le fauci e il petto del leone, il corpo d'asino, le corna di stambecco",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia digambarkan bersuara manusia, tetapi giginya berupa satu tulang utuh, mulutnya terbelah sampai telinga, berahang dan berdada singa, bertubuh keledai, dan bertanduk ibex.",
        "en": "It is described as having a human voice but a solid bone instead of teeth, a mouth slit to the ears, a lion's jaws and chest, an ass's body and ibex horns."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cenocroca-c03",
      "source_id": "cenocroca-s1",
      "quote": "Possedeva la bizzarra caratteristica di non muovere mai le pupille e di guardare sempre dinanzi a sé; con la propria voce umana imitava i richiami degli uomini o i versi degli animali per attrarre le prede e divorarle.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia tidak pernah menggerakkan pupilnya dan selalu menatap lurus ke depan, dan dengan suara manusianya ia meniru panggilan orang atau suara hewan untuk memikat mangsa lalu melahapnya.",
        "en": "It never moved its pupils and always stared straight ahead, and with its human voice it imitated people's calls or animal cries to lure prey and devour it."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cenocroca-c04",
      "source_id": "cenocroca-s1",
      "quote": "La più antica menzione di una creatura chiamata Κροκόττας si trova all'interno della Geografia di Strabone, dove l'animale è descritto come un incrocio fra un cane e un lupo nativo dell'Etiopia.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sebutan tertua makhluk bernama Krokottas ada dalam Geografi karya Strabo, sebagai persilangan anjing dan serigala dari Etiopia.",
        "en": "The oldest mention of a creature called Krokottas is in Strabo's Geography, describing it as a dog-wolf cross native to Ethiopia."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cenocroca-c05",
      "source_id": "cenocroca-s1",
      "quote": "La creatura viene descritta anche all'interno de Il nome della rosa di Umberto Eco dal monaco benedettino Adso da Melk.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Makhluk ini juga digambarkan dalam Il nome della rosa karya Umberto Eco oleh biarawan Benediktin Adso dari Melk.",
        "en": "The creature is also described in Umberto Eco's Il nome della rosa by the Benedictine monk Adso of Melk."
      }
    }
  ],

    "en": "The Coga is a vampire witch of the folk belief of Sardinia who creeps into houses at night to kill newborns.",

    {
      "id": "Dalam kepercayaan rakyat Sardinia tengah dan selatan, Coga atau Bruxa adalah penyihir atau perempuan vampir. Pada malam hari para Cogas menyelinap berkelompok ke rumah untuk membunuh bayi, terutama bayi lelaki. Orang tua menaruh rosario di buaian, karena Coga akan menghitung butirnya dan tak pernah selesai sebelum fajar.",
      "en": "In the folk beliefs of central-southern Sardinia, the Coga or Bruxa is a witch or vampire woman. At night the Cogas creep in groups into houses to kill newborns, mainly boys. Parents put a rosary on the cradle, since the Coga will count its beads and never finish before dawn.",

      "id": "Coga konon anak perempuan ketujuh dari keluarga yang hanya punya anak perempuan.       "en": "A coga is said to be the seventh daughter of a family of only girls. She looks like an old hag with a little tail from birth, and can turn into a cat or fly by anointing her joints with lard. Objects placed upside down drive her away because she dislikes disorder.",

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "coga-c01",
      "source_id": "coga-s1",
      "quote": "Nelle credenze popolari della Sardegna centro-meridionale, la Coga o Bruxa [‘bru:ʒa] (al plurale is Cogas o is Bruxas) era la cosiddetta strega, o una donna vampiro .",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam kepercayaan rakyat Sardinia tengah dan selatan, Coga atau Bruxa (jamak: is Cogas) adalah penyihir atau perempuan vampir.",
        "en": "In the popular beliefs of central-southern Sardinia, the Coga or Bruxa (plural is Cogas) was a witch or vampire woman."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "coga-c02",
      "source_id": "coga-s1",
      "quote": "Durante la notte, in gruppo, si intrufolavano nelle case dove c'erano dei neonati (di sesso maschile principalmente) per ucciderli.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada malam hari mereka berkelompok menyelinap ke rumah yang ada bayi, terutama bayi lelaki, untuk membunuhnya.",
        "en": "At night they crept in groups into houses with newborns, mainly boys, to kill them."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "coga-c03",
      "source_id": "coga-s1",
      "quote": "Queste creature tenebrose quando giungevano accanto alla culla iniziavano a contare i grani del rosario, senza però mai riuscire a contarli tutti prima dell'alba",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Orang tua menaruh tongkat buluh dan rosario yang diberkati di buaian; Coga yang datang mulai menghitung butir rosario tetapi tak pernah selesai sebelum fajar.",
        "en": "Parents laid a cane and a blessed rosary on the cradle; the Coga would start counting the rosary beads but never finish before dawn."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "coga-c04",
      "source_id": "coga-s1",
      "quote": "Si narra che una coga sia la settima figlia di una famiglia nella quale sono nate solo femmine.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Konon coga adalah anak perempuan ketujuh dalam keluarga yang hanya punya anak perempuan.",
        "en": "A coga is said to be the seventh daughter of a family of only girls."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "coga-c05",
      "source_id": "coga-s1",
      "quote": "Le Cogas avevano l'aspetto di vecchie megere ma si riconoscevano soprattutto perché avevano un piccolo pezzo di coda da quando erano nate e avevano la capacità di assumere qualsiasi forma.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Cogas tampak seperti nenek sihir tua, tetapi dikenali dari ekor kecil sejak lahir, dan dapat berubah menjadi wujud apa pun.",
        "en": "The Cogas looked like old hags but were recognised mainly by a little tail they had from birth, and could take any form."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "coga-c06",
      "source_id": "coga-s1",
      "quote": "Villacidro, capoluogo della Provincia del Medio Campidano, è rinomato per il suo appellativo: Sa bidda de is cogas.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Villacidro, ibu kota Provinsi Medio Campidano, terkenal dengan julukan Sa bidda de is cogas, \"desa para coga\".",
        "en": "Villacidro, capital of the Province of Medio Campidano, is famed for its nickname Sa bidda de is cogas, \"the village of the cogas\"."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "coga-c07",
      "source_id": "coga-s1",
      "quote": "si ungevano le giunture del corpo per trasformarsi in animali, gatti o mosche.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut Marcialis, mereka mengolesi sendi tubuhnya dengan lemak babi yang dilelehkan untuk berubah menjadi hewan, kucing, atau lalat.",
        "en": "According to Marcialis, they anointed their joints with melted lard to turn into animals, cats or flies."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "coga-c08",
      "source_id": "coga-s1",
      "quote": "Si diceva che la coga, non essendo amante del disordine, alla vista di questi oggetti si sarebbe allontanata o addirittura sarebbe morta.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Benda yang diletakkan terbalik di rumah, seperti sapu atau tungku berkaki tiga, mengusir coga karena ia tidak suka kekacauan dan bisa mati melihatnya.",
        "en": "Objects placed upside down in the house, such as a broom or trivet, drove off the coga, which disliked disorder and might even die at the sight."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "coga-c09",
      "source_id": "coga-s1",
      "quote": "La figura delle Cogas può essere associata a quella di Lilith antica divinità sumera, che per la cultura ebraica è un demone femminile",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sosok Cogas dapat dikaitkan dengan Lilith, dewi Sumeria kuno yang dalam budaya Yahudi adalah iblis perempuan.",
        "en": "The Cogas can be linked with Lilith, an ancient Sumerian deity who in Jewish culture is a female demon."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],

    "id": "Confinati adalah arwah gentayangan dalam tradisi Lombardia timur laut yang diusir lewat eksorsisme ke lembah terpencil.",
    "en": "The confinati are restless souls of north-eastern Lombard tradition, banished by exorcism to remote valleys.",
    "claim_ids": [

  },

    {
      "id": "Confinati atau anime confinate adalah tokoh mitos dalam tradisi rakyat Lombardia timur laut, terutama di lembah Bergamo, Val Camonica, dan Valtellina. Mereka arwah orang mati yang tidak tenang, yang diusir lewat eksorsisme ke lembah samping yang tak ramah agar tidak mengganggu orang hidup. Dalam bahasa Lombard mereka disebut cunfinàcc atau cunfinàa.",
      "en": "The confinati or anime confinate are mythical figures of the folk traditions of north-eastern Lombardy, especially the Bergamo valleys, Val Camonica and Valtellina. They are the souls of dissatisfied dead, banished by exorcism to inhospitable side valleys so they cannot harm the living. In Lombard they are called cunfinàcc or cunfinàa.",

      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Confinati atau anime confinate (\"jiwa terbuang\") adalah tokoh mitos dalam tradisi rakyat Lombardia timur laut, terutama di lembah Bergamo, Val Camonica, dan Valtellina.",
        "en": "The confinati or anime confinate (\"confined souls\") are mythical figures of the folk traditions of north-eastern Lombardy, especially the Bergamo valleys, Val Camonica and Valtellina."

    {
      "id": "confinati-c02",
      "source_id": "confinati-s1",
      "quote": "Essi erano delle anime di persone morte insoddisfatte, che erano state mandate al confino tramite un esorcismo in vallate laterali ed inospitali, in modo tale da non poter nuocere ai vivi.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mereka arwah orang mati yang tidak tenang, yang diusir lewat eksorsisme ke lembah samping yang tak ramah agar tidak mengganggu orang hidup.",
        "en": "They were the souls of dissatisfied dead, banished by exorcism to remote, inhospitable side valleys so they could not harm the living."
      }
    },
    {

      "source_id": "confinati-s1",
      "quote": "In lombardo sono chiamati cunfinàcc (orientale) cunfinàa (occidentale).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam bahasa Lombard mereka disebut cunfinàcc (timur) atau cunfinàa (barat).",

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Crieur atau appeleur adalah makhluk malam cerita rakyat yang berteriak memanggil pejalan, untuk menjebak atau memperingatkan mereka.",
    "en": "Crieurs or appeleurs are nocturnal folklore creatures that call out to travellers, to trap them or to warn them.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "crieur-c01"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Crieur atau appeleur adalah sekelompok makhluk malam yang sama-sama berteriak atau memanggil pejalan, untuk menjebak atau memperingatkan bahaya. Mereka umumnya digambarkan sebagai lutin atau arwah dan dapat berwujud manusia atau hewan. Namanya berbeda di tiap daerah Prancis: lupeux di Berry, houppeux di Picardie, crieux di Maine dan Anjou, hueur di Limousin.",
      "en": "The crieurs or appeleurs are a group of nocturnal creatures that call or shout to travellers, to lure them into traps or warn them of danger. They are usually described as lutins or revenants and can take human or animal form. Their names vary by region: lupeux in Berry, houppeux in Picardy, crieux in Maine and Anjou, hueur in the Limousin.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "crieur-c01",
        "crieur-c03",
        "crieur-c04"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Hupeur adalah burung malam di dekat rawa yang berteriak dengan suara manusia, memikat pejalan ke lumpur, lalu berubah wujud untuk menenggelamkan mereka. Lupeux di Berry, yang disebut George Sand, terus berseru sampai dijawab. Moine de Saire berpura-pura tenggelam di teluk Cherbourg untuk menyeret pelaut ke dasar laut.",
      "en": "The hupeur is a night bird of the marshes that cries with a human voice, lures walkers into the mud and changes shape to drown them. The lupeux of Berry, mentioned by George Sand, calls out until someone answers. The moine de Saire pretends to drown in the Cherbourg roadstead to drag sailors under.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "crieur-c05",
        "crieur-c06",
        "crieur-c07",
        "crieur-c08"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Pierre Dubois menganggap mereka bentara dunia peri. Legenda ini ada di mana-mana dengan tema malam dan bahayanya.",
      "en": "Pierre Dubois sees them as heralds of fairyland. The legend is found everywhere, centred on night and its dangers.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "crieur-c02",
        "crieur-c09"
      ]
    }

      "quote": "Les crieurs, ou appeleurs, forment un ensemble de créatures nocturnes issues de la mythologie et du folklore populaire, dont la caractéristique commune est de crier ou d'apostropher les voyageurs, soit pour les attirer dans des pièges, soit pour les avertir d'un danger.",

      "statement": {
        "id": "Crieur atau appeleur adalah sekelompok makhluk malam dalam mitologi dan cerita rakyat yang sama-sama berteriak atau memanggil pejalan, entah untuk menjebak atau memperingatkan bahaya.",
        "en": "The crieurs or appeleurs are a group of nocturnal creatures of myth and folklore whose shared trait is shouting or calling to travellers, either to lure them into traps or to warn them of danger."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "crieur-c02",
      "source_id": "crieur-s1",
      "quote": "Pierre Dubois pense qu'il n'en est rien, et donne une explication de conteur : les appeleurs seraient les hérauts du monde de féerie, leur mission consistant à prévenir les hommes de leur folie meurtrière.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pierre Dubois memberi penjelasan ala pendongeng: para appeleur adalah bentara dunia peri yang bertugas memperingatkan manusia akan kegilaan mereka yang mematikan.",
        "en": "Pierre Dubois offers a storyteller's explanation: the appeleurs are heralds of fairyland whose mission was to warn humans of their murderous folly."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "crieur-c03",
      "source_id": "crieur-s1",
      "quote": "Généralement décrits comme des lutins ou des revenants, ils peuvent revêtir de très nombreuses formes humanoïdes ou animales.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mereka umumnya digambarkan sebagai lutin atau arwah, dan dapat mengambil banyak wujud manusia atau hewan.",
        "en": "Usually described as lutins or revenants, they can take many human or animal forms."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "crieur-c04",
      "source_id": "crieur-s1",
      "quote": "Le nom employé est hupeu ou lupeux dans le Berry, houppeux en Picardie, houpou en Haute-Bretagne, crieux dans le Maine et l'Anjou, hueur dans le Limousin",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Namanya hupeu atau lupeux di Berry, houppeux di Picardie, houpou di Haute-Bretagne, crieux di Maine dan Anjou, dan hueur di Limousin.",
        "en": "They are called hupeu or lupeux in Berry, houppeux in Picardy, houpou in Haute-Bretagne, crieux in Maine and Anjou, and hueur in the Limousin."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "crieur-c05",
      "source_id": "crieur-s1",
      "quote": "Le hupeur, ou hueur, est un oiseau nocturne fabuleux du folklore français, qui niche près des étangs et des marais. Lorsque la nuit tombe, il se met à crier d'une voix humaine pour attirer les promeneurs égarés.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Hupeur atau hueur adalah burung malam khayalan dalam cerita rakyat Prancis yang bersarang dekat kolam dan rawa; saat malam tiba ia berteriak dengan suara manusia untuk memikat pejalan yang tersesat.",
        "en": "The hupeur or hueur is a fabulous night bird of French folklore that nests near ponds and marshes; at nightfall it cries with a human voice to lure lost walkers."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "crieur-c06",
      "source_id": "crieur-s1",
      "quote": "Il se transforme alors pour pousser et noyer l'imprudent, puis reprend son apparence première afin de trouver une autre victime.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia lalu berubah wujud untuk mendorong dan menenggelamkan orang yang lengah, kemudian kembali ke wujud semula untuk mencari korban lain.",
        "en": "It then changes shape to push and drown the careless victim, before returning to its first form to find another."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "crieur-c07",
      "source_id": "crieur-s1",
      "quote": "George Sand évoque cette créature dans ses légendes rustiques : dans le Berry, le lupeux répète Ah ! Ah ! jusqu'à ce qu'on lui réponde.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "George Sand menyebut makhluk ini dalam legenda pedesaannya: di Berry, lupeux terus berseru \"Ah! Ah!\" sampai ada yang menjawab.",
        "en": "George Sand mentions the creature in her rustic legends: in Berry the lupeux repeats \"Ah! Ah!\" until someone answers."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "crieur-c08",
      "source_id": "crieur-s1",
      "quote": "Le moine de Saire a l'apparence d'un petit homme en frac blanc. Tantôt, il apparaît dans la rade de Cherbourg où il semble se noyer, suppliant les matelots et les marins de l'aider.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Moine de Saire tampak seperti lelaki kecil berjas putih; kadang ia muncul di teluk Cherbourg seolah tenggelam dan memohon pertolongan para pelaut.",
        "en": "The moine de Saire looks like a little man in a white tailcoat; sometimes he appears in the Cherbourg roadstead as if drowning, begging sailors for help."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "crieur-c09",
      "source_id": "crieur-s1",
      "quote": "Cette légende est universellement présente sous diverses variantes qui ont pour point commun la nuit et ses dangers.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Legenda ini ada di mana-mana dalam berbagai versi yang sama-sama berkisar pada malam dan bahayanya.",
        "en": "The legend is found everywhere in many variants sharing the theme of night and its dangers."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],
  "gaps": [
    {
      "field": "sources",
      "searched": "Hanya tersedia artikel Wikipedia fr untuk makhluk ini; belum ditemukan sumber kedua yang dapat dikutip."

    {
      "field": "images",

    }
  ]
}

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Cucala adalah makhluk tak berbentuk dalam mitologi Katalan yang berjaga di jalan malam hari dan memukul siapa pun yang ditemuinya.",
    "en": "The cucala is a shapeless creature of Catalan mythology that patrols the streets at night and beats anyone it meets.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "cucala-c01",
      "cucala-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Cucala, cucula, atau cucal adalah makhluk tak berbentuk jelas dari Terres de l'Ebre, Camp de Tarragona, dan Mallorca, setara dengan gambosí. Di Camp de Tarragona ia berjaga di jalan pada malam hari dan memukul keras siapa pun yang lewat atau melongok dari jendela. Tokoh serupa di Ribera d'Ebre, feram, mengganggu perempuan dengan menarik rok mereka.",
      "en": "The cucala, cucula or cucal is a shapeless creature of the Terres de l'Ebre, the Camp de Tarragona and Mallorca, equivalent to the gambosí. In the Camp de Tarragona it patrols the streets at night and beats anyone passing or looking out of a window. A similar figure of the Ribera d'Ebre, the feram, harassed women by tugging their skirts.",

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "cucala-c01",
      "source_id": "cucala-s1",
      "quote": "Una cucala, cucula o cucal és, dins la mitologia catalana, una criatura de forma indefinida equivalent al gambosí pròpia de les Terres de l'Ebre, el Camp de Tarragona i Mallorca.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam mitologi Katalan, cucala, cucula, atau cucal adalah makhluk tak berbentuk jelas yang setara dengan gambosí, dari Terres de l'Ebre, Camp de Tarragona, dan Mallorca.",
        "en": "In Catalan mythology a cucala, cucula or cucal is a shapeless creature equivalent to the gambosí, found in the Terres de l'Ebre, the Camp de Tarragona and Mallorca."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cucala-c02",
      "source_id": "cucala-s1",
      "quote": "Al Camp de Tarragona, hom creia que la cucala vigilava pels carrers de nit i pegava ben fort tothom que hi trobés a aquelles hores, ja fos passant-hi o traient el cap per la finestra.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Camp de Tarragona orang percaya cucala berjaga di jalan pada malam hari dan memukul keras siapa pun yang ditemuinya, entah lewat atau melongok dari jendela.",
        "en": "In the Camp de Tarragona people believed the cucala watched the streets at night and beat hard anyone it found there, whether passing by or looking out of a window."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cucala-c03",
      "source_id": "cucala-s1",
      "quote": "Un personatge semblant i propi de la Ribera d'Ebre, la feram, estirava les faldilles de les dones i els deia paraules deshonestes",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Tokoh serupa di Ribera d'Ebre, feram, menarik rok perempuan dan mengucapkan kata-kata tak senonoh.",
        "en": "A similar figure of the Ribera d'Ebre, the feram, tugged women's skirts and said indecent things to them."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Dames blanches adalah roh perempuan berpakaian putih dalam cerita rakyat Prancis yang menghadang pejalan di jurang, jembatan, atau jalan.",
    "en": "The Dames blanches are white-clad female spirits of French folklore who waylay travellers in ravines, on bridges or on roads.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "dames-blanches-c01",
      "dames-blanches-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Dames Blanches, \"Perempuan Putih\", adalah roh dalam cerita rakyat Prancis yang sebanding dengan Weiße Frauen Belanda dan Jerman. Mereka perempuan hantu berpakaian serba putih yang ditemui di jurang, jembatan, atau tepi jalan. Mereka dapat meminta pejalan menari atau menolong mereka sebelum boleh lewat, atau memaksa menari sampai mati. Dalam versi lain mereka menjaga harta di kastel atau menjadi pertanda kematian.",
      "en": "The Dames Blanches, \"White Ladies\", are spirits of French folklore comparable to the Dutch and German Weiße Frauen. They are spectral women dressed all in white, found in ravines, on bridges or along roads. They may require travellers to dance with or help them before passing, or force them to dance to death. In other versions they guard treasure in castles or foretell death.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "dames-blanches-c01",
        "dames-blanches-c02",
        "dames-blanches-c03",
        "dames-blanches-c04"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "La Dame d'Apringy di Bayeux meminta orang menari dengannya, dan Dame di Pont d'Angot hanya membolehkan orang lewat bila berlutut. Di Quebec, Dame Blanche di Air Terjun Montmorency diyakini arwah Mathilde, yang terjun dengan gaun pengantin setelah tunangannya gugur.",
      "en": "La Dame d'Apringy at Bayeux made people dance with her, and the Dame of the Pont d'Angot let people pass only if they knelt. In Quebec the Dame Blanche of Montmorency Falls is believed to be Mathilde, who leapt in her wedding gown after her fiancé fell in battle.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "dames-blanches-c05",
        "dames-blanches-c06",
        "dames-blanches-c07",
        "dames-blanches-c08"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "J. A. MacCulloch melihat mereka sebagai bentuk baru dewi-dewi pra-Kristen, mungkin dewi penjaga yang disebut Matres.",
      "en": "J. A. MacCulloch sees them as a recasting of pre-Christian goddesses, perhaps the guardian goddesses called the Matres.",
      "claim_ids": [

      ]
    }

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "dames-blanches-c01",
      "source_id": "dames-blanches-s1",
      "quote": "In French mythology or folklore, Dames Blanches (meaning literally White Ladies) are spirits frequently seen in French folklore and horror, comparable to the Weiße Frauen of both Dutch and German mythology.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam mitologi atau cerita rakyat Prancis, Dames Blanches (\"Perempuan Putih\") adalah roh yang sering muncul dalam cerita rakyat dan kisah seram Prancis, sebanding dengan Weiße Frauen dalam mitologi Belanda dan Jerman.",
        "en": "In French mythology or folklore, the Dames Blanches (literally White Ladies) are spirits often found in French folklore and horror, comparable to the Weiße Frauen of Dutch and German mythology."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "dames-blanches-c02",
      "source_id": "dames-blanches-s1",
      "quote": "Dames Blanches are most commonly described as spectral women wearing all white. They are sometimes considered a type of malevolent Fée or ghost and can be found in ravines, on bridges, or along roads.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mereka paling sering digambarkan sebagai perempuan hantu berpakaian serba putih, kadang dianggap sejenis peri (fée) jahat atau hantu, dan ditemui di jurang, jembatan, atau tepi jalan.",
        "en": "They are most often described as spectral women dressed all in white, sometimes considered a kind of malevolent fée or ghost, found in ravines, on bridges or along roads."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "dames-blanches-c03",
      "source_id": "dames-blanches-s1",
      "quote": "A Dame Blanche may require passerby to dance with or help her in some way before they are allowed to go on safely. In other versions, the Dame Blanche will force the passerby to dance until they die of exhaustion.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dame Blanche dapat meminta pejalan menari dengannya atau menolongnya sebelum boleh lewat dengan selamat; dalam versi lain ia memaksa pejalan menari sampai mati kelelahan.",
        "en": "A Dame Blanche may require passers-by to dance with her or help her before letting them pass safely; in other versions she forces them to dance until they die of exhaustion."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "dames-blanches-c04",
      "source_id": "dames-blanches-s1",
      "quote": "Another version of Dames Blanches are as spirits that protect treasure in castles. They are often considered a harbinger of death.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam versi lain mereka roh penjaga harta di kastel, dan sering dianggap pertanda kematian.",
        "en": "In another version they are spirits guarding treasure in castles, and they are often considered harbingers of death."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "dames-blanches-c05",
      "source_id": "dames-blanches-s1",
      "quote": "La Dame d'Apringy is described as a Dame Blanche who appeared in a ravine at the Rue Quentin at Bayeux in Normandy, where one must dance with her a few rounds to pass.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "La Dame d'Apringy adalah Dame Blanche yang muncul di jurang di Rue Quentin, Bayeux, Normandia, tempat orang harus menari beberapa putaran dengannya agar boleh lewat.",
        "en": "La Dame d'Apringy is a Dame Blanche who appeared in a ravine on the Rue Quentin at Bayeux in Normandy, where one had to dance a few rounds with her to pass."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "dames-blanches-c06",
      "source_id": "dames-blanches-s1",
      "quote": "Another Dame was known on a narrow bridge in the district of Falaise, named the Pont d'Angot. She only allowed people to pass if they went on their knees to her.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dame lain ada di jembatan sempit Pont d'Angot di distrik Falaise, yang hanya membolehkan orang lewat bila berlutut kepadanya.",
        "en": "Another Dame haunted a narrow bridge, the Pont d'Angot in the district of Falaise, letting people pass only if they knelt to her."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "dames-blanches-c07",
      "source_id": "dames-blanches-s1",
      "quote": "In Quebec, there are stories of a Dame Blanche at Montmorency Falls. It is believed that this is the spirit of a young woman named Mathilde.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Quebec ada kisah Dame Blanche di Air Terjun Montmorency, yang diyakini arwah seorang perempuan muda bernama Mathilde.",
        "en": "In Quebec there are stories of a Dame Blanche at Montmorency Falls, believed to be the spirit of a young woman named Mathilde."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "dames-blanches-c08",
      "source_id": "dames-blanches-s1",
      "quote": "Distraught, she threw herself over the falls while still in her wedding gown.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Setelah menemukan tunangannya tewas di medan perang, Mathilde yang putus asa terjun dari air terjun dengan gaun pengantinnya.",
        "en": "After finding her fiancé dead on the battlefield, the distraught Mathilde threw herself over the falls in her wedding gown."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "dames-blanches-c09",
      "source_id": "dames-blanches-s1",
      "quote": "MacCulloch believes Dames Blanches are one of the recharacterizations of pre-Christian female goddesses, and suggested their name Dame may have derived from the ancient guardian goddesses known as the Matres",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "J. A. MacCulloch menganggap Dames Blanches salah satu bentuk baru dewi-dewi pra-Kristen, dan menduga nama Dame berasal dari dewi penjaga kuno yang disebut Matres.",
        "en": "J. A. MacCulloch thinks the Dames Blanches are a recasting of pre-Christian goddesses, suggesting the name Dame derives from the ancient guardian goddesses called the Matres."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Iblis berjari besi adalah iblis penyakit paru dalam mitologi Cherokee yang mengisap paru-paru dan hati korban lewat jari besinya.",
    "en": "The iron-fingered demon is a demon of consumption in Cherokee mythology that sucks out victims' lungs and liver with its iron finger.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "demone-dal-dito-di-ferro-c01",
      "demone-dal-dito-di-ferro-c02",
      "demone-dal-dito-di-ferro-c03"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Dalam mitologi Cherokee, iblis berjari besi atau iblis penyakit paru adalah iblis pengubah wujud yang tinggal di gua. Menurut versi yang dicatat di Carolina Utara pada 1892, ia menyamar sebagai kerabat yang sedang bepergian, menidurkan anggota keluarganya, lalu mengisap paru-paru dan hatinya lewat lubang yang dibuat dengan jari besinya. Korban bangun dengan penyakit tak dikenal, makin kurus, lalu meninggal.",
      "en": "In Cherokee mythology the iron-fingered demon or demon of consumption is a shape-changing demon living in a cave. In a version recorded in North Carolina in 1892, it takes the form of an absent relative, puts a family member to sleep, and sucks out their lungs and liver through a hole made with its iron finger. The victim wakes with an unknown illness, wastes away and dies.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "demone-dal-dito-di-ferro-c01",
        "demone-dal-dito-di-ferro-c02",
        "demone-dal-dito-di-ferro-c03",
        "demone-dal-dito-di-ferro-c04"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Para pemimpin Cherokee memburunya tanpa hasil sampai seekor burung kecil menyuruh mereka membidik jari besinya, satu-satunya titik lemahnya, dan iblis itu pun lenyap. Iblis-iblis serupa kemudian muncul lagi, tetapi tanpa jari besi.",
      "en": "The Cherokee chiefs hunted it in vain until a little bird told them to aim at its iron finger, its only weak spot, and the demon vanished. Similar demons later arose again, but without the iron finger.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "demone-dal-dito-di-ferro-c05",
        "demone-dal-dito-di-ferro-c06"
      ]
    }
